# D2 naive fine-tuning — continuous experiment log
#
# This is the only hyperparameter/experiment log for this workflow. Do not
# replace prior entries. Append every launch, diagnosis, code/default change,
# and planned next test here. Each launch record must identify its run folder,
# training log, checkpoint model, compiled model (if available), command, and
# resolved hyperparameters.

2026-09-29T00:00:00-04:00 — Dataset-audit tooling added
observation=The active D2 target_all.xyz contains 227 formal-charge-verified H/N/O DFT frames, whereas the parallel D_MHFT target data is an older 162-frame oxygen-only dataset. The former is the candidate authoritative dataset; the latter must not be selected for the nitrogen-bearing target workflow.
diagnosis_or_uncertainty=The existing seeded formula/charge/spin stratification is reproducible, but rare formula strata leave only one nitrogen-bearing frame in each 11-frame validation/test split. This requires a chemistry-balance review before regenerating splits.
code_change=Added analyze_dataset_distribution.py, a read-only audit that reports element/frame balance, formulas, charge/spin/source distributions, formal-charge mismatches, exact geometry duplicates, cross-split leakage, and partition completeness.
proposed_next_test=Run the audit on the current D2 all/train/valid/test files, then decide the desired validation/test chemistry representation and whether a fixed source-group holdout is needed.
why_most_informative=It separates demonstrable split defects from choices about the intended scientific generalization before any DFT data or historical workflow is removed.
changed_factor=dataset audit instrumentation only; no training or dataset content changed.

2026-09-29T00:00:00-04:00 — Training provenance logging strengthened
observation=The launcher already created unique run directories and recorded a pre-launch command, but the continuous log did not automatically distinguish completed from failed attempts or bind a run to immutable input-file contents and source revision.
diagnosis_or_uncertainty=Without terminal status and data hashes, a later analysis can mistake an interrupted run for a completed one or cannot prove which exact split files it consumed.
code_change=trainmace.py now writes run_metadata.json before optimization with train/validation SHA-256 hashes, Git revision when available, interpreter, CUDA visibility, cache path, resolved arguments, and command. It appends RUN END with elapsed time and completed/failed status on normal Python-level completion or exception.
proposed_next_test=No scientific training test selected; this is operational provenance only.
why_most_informative=It makes every future training attempt auditable without changing model, data, loss, optimizer, or scheduler behavior.
changed_factor=training-run audit logging only.

2026-09-29T00:00:00-04:00 — 80/10/10 split regenerated and delta artifacts archived
evidence=prepare_data.py --skip-orca --source-target-all data/target_all.xyz --seed 3 retained all 227 formal-charge-valid frames and wrote 181 train, 23 validation, and 23 test frames. analyze_dataset_distribution.py reports N-bearing frames=36/5/4, C3-source frames=52/7/6, zero duplicate geometries, zero cross-split overlaps, and zero frames missing from the partition.
diagnosis=The active split now represents the N-bearing C3 family in both holdouts, unlike the preserved 205/11/11 split. Charge distributions remain reported explicitly because rare individual charge states cannot all be represented in 23-frame holdouts.
data_change=Archived the exact former 205/11/11 files with SHA-256 manifest under data/archive/split_seed3_formula_charge_spin_90_5_5_2026-09-29. Moved only the identified water-only delta artifacts to data/archive/water_only_delta_experiments_2026-09-29; no raw DFT data or historical old/archive directories were removed.
next_one_factor_test=Establish a new full-training baseline using the active 80/10/10 split before altering optimizer, model, loss, learning rate, precision, replay, or scheduling.
why_most_informative=The split policy is the sole scientific change relative to earlier D2 runs; a baseline isolates its effect.
changed_factor=dataset split policy only.

2026-09-29T00:00:00-04:00 — MACE-POLAR embedded E0s made explicit
observation=A dry-run of the active D2 launcher resolves --E0s=foundation and --foundation_model=polar-1-s. The prior data-preparation default still wrote a custom target_dft_e0s.json even though active training did not use it.
diagnosis=The standard D2 workflow should retain the MACE-POLAR embedded atomic-energy references because its DFT standard matches the foundation model; writing an unused custom table risks later confusion.
code_change=prepare_data.py now writes a custom DFT E0 JSON only with explicit --write-dft-e0s. README.md now states that foundation E0s are required for standard D2 training and that alternate E0 modes are for separately declared experiments only.
next_one_factor_test=No new training hyperparameter test is selected. The next scientific run remains the 80/10/10 split baseline using E0s=foundation.
why_most_informative=This changes only provenance/default handling of an unused custom reference artifact; it does not alter the active foundation E0 setting, model, data, or loss.
changed_factor=E0 provenance/default handling only.

2026-09-04T09:52:00-04:00 — Analysis of latest full baseline and planned child run
parent_run=/home/kevinsh/mlip/codes/D2_naive/runs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T093646_611434-0400

evidence=Validation force RMSE fell from 250.67 meV/A initially to 181.80 meV/A at epoch 14. Stage Two began at epoch 15, changing the loss weights and increasing learning rate from 1e-4 to swa_lr=1e-3. Force RMSE then reached its minimum observed value, 149.47 meV/A, at epoch 74 and fluctuated around 150–153 meV/A thereafter. The run log ends at epoch 76 without a Training complete entry.
evidence_energy=Validation energy RMSE rose from 47.65 meV/atom at epoch 14 to 58.97 at epoch 15 and 109.50 at epoch 76. This is contemporaneous with the Stage-Two transition.
diagnosis=The 10-fold Stage-Two learning-rate increase is the most direct suspected cause of energy drift and the force plateau. The expanded N-containing chemistry remains difficult, but this schedule discontinuity is the first isolated factor to test.
next_one_factor_test=Reduce only swa_lr from 0.001 to 0.0001; retain Stage Two, all loss weights, data files, model, Adam, EMA, seed, and all other settings. Train for 100 epochs on GPU 1.
why=This tests the observed instability at the exact schedule transition without confounding it with a model, loss, data, or optimizer change. A 100-epoch run covers 85 Stage-Two epochs, more than the parent reached.
changed_parameter=swa_learning_rate
child_command=/home/kevinsh/env/bin/python launch_single_gpu.py --gpu 1 -- --max-num-epochs 100 --swa-lr 0.0001 --changed-parameter swa_learning_rate --change-note "Lower only Stage-Two LR from 1e-3 to 1e-4 to reduce post-epoch-15 energy drift and force plateau."

2026-09-04T10:15:00-04:00 — Analysis of swa_lr=1e-4 child and course correction
child_run=/home/kevinsh/mlip/codes/D2_naive/runs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T095542_785890-0400
child_training_log=/home/kevinsh/mlip/codes/D2_naive/runs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T095542_785890-0400/logs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T095542_785890-0400_run-3.log
evidence=At matched epoch 31, the 1e-4 child has validation RMSE_F=177.67 meV/A and RMSE_E_per_atom=50.61 meV, while the 1e-3 parent has RMSE_F=155.81 meV/A and RMSE_E_per_atom=93.95 meV. Through epoch 33, the child force RMSE remains 176–178 meV/A, substantially worse than the parent force trajectory, while its energy remains controlled near 50.7 meV/atom.
diagnosis=Reducing swa_lr to 1e-4 removed the high-LR energy drift but made Stage Two underfit the force correction. The two prior values bracket the desired tradeoff: 1e-3 learns forces faster but destabilizes energy; 1e-4 stabilizes energy but learns forces too slowly.
next_one_factor_test=Set only swa_lr to the geometric intermediate 3e-4. Retain the same model, data, loss weights, EMA, Stage-Two start, seed, GPU 1, and 100-epoch duration.
why=An intermediate learning rate is the direct one-dimensional course correction supported by the two bracketing trajectories. It tests whether force progress can recover without the full energy drift seen at 1e-3.
changed_parameter=swa_learning_rate
code_change=trainmace.py active default changed from swa_lr=1e-4 to swa_lr=3e-4; README.md synchronized. The user can run the prepared test with no arguments.

2026-09-22 analysis: latest completed fine-tuning run is polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T101538_510780-0400 (100 epochs; swa_learning_rate=3e-4). Parsed its training log and wrote plots at runs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T101538_510780-0400/results/loss_by_epoch.png, runs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T101538_510780-0400/results/energy_error_by_epoch.png, and runs/polar1s_naive_orca_dft_e0_20260904T101538_510780-0400/results/force_error_by_epoch.png. Evidence: loss is 4.5504 at epoch 0, reaches its stage-one minimum 3.6546 at epoch 3, and is 3131.7134 at epoch 99; the discontinuity reflects the logged switch to the different, heavily weighted SWA objective. Energy RMSE per atom increases from 24.27 to 65.70 meV; force RMSE decreases from 200.72 to its minimum 163.70 meV/A at epoch 93 (164.36 meV/A at epoch 99). Diagnosis / uncertainty: the epoch metrics trade energy accuracy for force accuracy after SWA begins; these are the training-loop epoch metrics, while final validation metrics for the selected stage-two checkpoint are 65.3 meV/atom and 163.7 meV/A. Next one-factor test: not selected in this plotting-only analysis. Changed factor relative to this run: none.

ANALYSIS 2026-10-27T06:51:28-04:00
- evidence: An exhaustive audit of the active cycle2 snapshot found 358/358 source frames and 251/251 training plus 107/107 validation frames with extxyz charge exactly equal to the recomputed formal charge under H=+1, N=-3, O=-2, S=-2; every frame has spin=1 and an even electron count. All 500 P31 ORCA inputs match their manifest charge, recomputed formal charge, atom count, and multiplicity 1. The live MACE log reports total_charge: 251 and total_spin: 251 for training and total_charge: 107 and total_spin: 107 for validation, using keys charge and spin.
- diagnosis / uncertainty: The active batch inputs are charge/spin consistent, but D2_naive/trainmace.py previously trusted extxyz metadata after only checking that the files existed. Campaign collection was strict, yet a future direct or alternate-wrapper launch could bypass recomputation immediately before training.
- next one-factor test: Add a pre-run launcher gate that recomputes every frame's formal charge, requires exact extxyz charge agreement, requires spin multiplicity 1, rejects unsupported elements, and checks even electron parity before creating a run directory.
- why this test is informative: It converts the successful manual audit into a global fail-closed invariant at the final MLIP entry point without changing the active model, dataset, split, optimizer, loss, or any other training setting.
- changed factor relative to the active P31 run: Input validation enforcement only; scientific and training parameters are unchanged.

Their processed datasets each contain 34 accepted ORCA structures: 33 are exact symbol/position/energy/force duplicates; the legacy accepted set uniquely contains cluster_038.out and Cycle2 uniquely contains cluster_044.out. In the active 358-row split, 19 exact duplicate pairs are both in training, 3 are both in validation, and 11 cross the train/validation boundary. Code tracing shows inherit-dft snapshots only processed.extxyz files (campaign.py lines 267-281), subsequent cluster exclusion reads only the named run's local clusters/acquisition*/selection.csv ledgers (lines 928-940), and fine tuning merges datasets by distinct file path before shuffling individual rows without a structure-identity check (lines 1433-1455).
- diagnosis / uncertainty: This is a workflow provenance bug, not coincidental duplicate chemistry. Cycle2 inherited the completed legacy acquisition labels without inheriting their selection ledger. The later acquisition_r4 therefore treated those already-labelled event components as unseen, reselected the identical 50 events, and reran ORCA. Because file-path uniqueness was mistaken for configuration uniqueness, both datasets entered training and 11 identical labels leaked across the random split. Every Cycle2 fine-tuning snapshot that included acquisition_r4 is scientifically contaminated by duplicate weighting and, where duplicates crossed the split, invalid validation independence. The exact numerical effect on each historical model remains unquantified.
- next one-factor test: Before any new fine tuning, repair dataset construction to reject duplicate structure/label identities across all local and inherited sources, then regenerate a deduplicated split from the same existing DFT snapshot and reevaluate the existing parent/model baselines without changing model or optimizer settings. Do not launch this test until the user approves the repair and disposition of contaminated lineage.
- why this test is informative: Holding the underlying unique DFT configurations and training recipe fixed isolates the effect of duplicate weighting and train/validation leakage while establishing an auditable independent validation set.
- changed factor relative to this run: dataset identity policy only (path-based concatenation to fail-closed structure/label deduplication).
